- **design-system-auditor**: Audits a codebase or design files for design system drift, inconsistency, and token coverage. - **design-system-auditor**: Design system accessibility auditor. Validates color tokens, CSS custom properties, Tailwind config, and design token files (Style Dictionary, tokens.json) for WCAG AA/AAA contrast compliance. Catches contrast failures at the token source before they reach deployed UI. Also validates focus ring tokens (WCAG 2.4.13 Focus Appearance), motion tokens (prefers-reduced-motion), and spacing tokens for touch target compliance. Supports MUI, Chakra UI, Radix, shadcn/ui, and Style Dictionary. ([TikeDev/simmer](https://github.com/TikeDev/simmer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-system-auditor.md) · ★2 · 10 copies)
- **design-system-auditor**: Audits UI changes against the project design system: tokens only, spacing, visual noise, tiers, sealed fundamentals, docs and stories, states in every context, accessibility and copy rules. - **design-system-auditor**: Read-only, grep-based design-system auditor. USE THIS AGENT WHEN: - Client has shared inspiration, moodboards, or reference sites - User says "design brief", "visual direction", "design system", "look and feel" - Preparing for design handoff or wireframing - Defining typography, color, spacing systems - Creating component specifications - Translating brand to web design language REQUIRES: Client DNA + Strategic Sitemap (preferred) + Insp… ([createdbysalt/nextjs-starter-template](https://github.com/createdbysalt/nextjs-starter-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-translator.md) · 2 copies)
- **design-triage**: Use this agent for the per-slice design gate. It decides whether a vertical slice needs UI components built (and which), or whether it fast-passes — either because it has no UI surface or because the existing design inventory already covers it. Trigger when the kernel's cf_next_step returns a triage step for a DesignTriage work item. It raises a recommendation; the human makes the final call before cf_triage_submit. ([jwilger/claude-factory](https://github.com/jwilger/claude-factory/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude-factory/agents/design-triage.md))
- **design-trigger**: &gt; Managed by `{{command}} init` and refreshed by `{{command}} migrate` — edit &gt; `templates/agents/design-trigger.md` in the Spektacular source, not this file in &gt; place. Hand edits will not survive the next init. Agents read this file &gt; when its rule's short section in `AGENTS.md` sends them here, and never edit it. ([hivecommons/spektacular](https://github.com/hivecommons/spektacular/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/design-trigger.md) · ★25)

Use to add or change a design token, spec a new reusable component at the system level, or audit cross-product consistency of the system itself. - **design-ui-designer**: Bevox UI Designer — turns the UX Architect's foundation and the Brand Guardian's identity into concrete components with development-ready specs (shadcn/ui on web, React Native on mobile). Accessibility is mandatory on every component. Invoked for: component spec, variant selection, handoff annotation, accessibility review of a component. Executes visual components; product-designer owns discovery and product flow. ([bernardTavares/bevox-blueprint](https://github.com/bernardTavares/bevox-blueprint/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-ui-designer.md))
- **design-ui-designer**: UI designer for John Aaron's portfolio. Turns critique findings into a readable visual system that keeps the neo-brutalist retro identity - typography scale, spacing rhythm, hierarchy and mobile layouts - and designs concrete alternatives (heroes, project cards, case-study layouts) as rendered mockups plus specs. Use when a critique says the look needs work, before the coder builds a visual change. ([johnaaronmendoza/portfolio-site](https://github.com/johnaaronmendoza/portfolio-site/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-ui-designer.md))
- **design-ui-designer**: Creates visual designs, layouts, and design system components to produce beautiful, consistent interfaces ([MonumentalSystems/Atlas-Agent-Teams](https://github.com/MonumentalSystems/Atlas-Agent-Teams/blob/HEAD/teams/design-ux/agents/ui-designer.md))

Use when building or restyling UI from a screenshot, a design description, the storebook reference, or an existing prototype. &lt;example&gt; user: "Build the item-detail card from this screenshot." assistant: uses design-ui-engineer to map the design onto existing base primitives + @theme tokens, produce a granular Vue component with a variant map and computed-only template, and type-check it. &lt;/example&gt; ([Mikssxed/tidansu](https://github.com/Mikssxed/tidansu/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-ui-engineer.md))
- **design-ui-finish-gate-reviewer**: Ekranın yayına çıkmadan önce son kalite kontrolü. ([GktuOktay/claude-agency](https://github.com/GktuOktay/claude-agency/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-ui-finish-gate-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **design-ui-finish-gate-reviewer**: Product-interface reviewer who catches generic, interchangeable UI before it ships by grounding critique in real product evidence, a written design contract, and a hard implementation finish gate. ([farzanshibu/foodos-platform](https://github.com/farzanshibu/foodos-platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-ui-finish-gate-reviewer.md) · 3 copies)
- **design-ui-finish-gate-reviewer-zh**: 产品界面审查员，通过基于真实产品证据、书面设计契约和硬性实现成品门槛的批评，在平庸、千篇一律的 UI 发布之前将其拦截。 ([RabbitAI-Lab/rabbit-plugins-experts](https://github.com/RabbitAI-Lab/rabbit-plugins-experts/blob/HEAD/plugins/design-ui-finish-gate-reviewer-zh/agents/design-ui-finish-gate-reviewer-zh.md))
- **design-ui-reviewer**: Readonly interface review of rendered screens: hierarchy, states, responsive and accessibility criteria, each finding classified as defect or improvement. Use for "revisa la interfaz" or "audita la UI". ([harrypotter48/rigorkit](https://github.com/harrypotter48/rigorkit/blob/HEAD/packs/design/agents/design-ui-reviewer.md) · ★11)
- **design-ultra**: Ultra design orchestrator. Runs a gap-analysis subagent, interviews the user one question at a time, drafts the design via a subagent, adversarially reviews, and iterates to approval. Writes one file to notes/design/. A single invocation runs the full loop. ([tianhuil/dotfiles](https://github.com/tianhuil/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/home/opencode/.config/opencode/agents/design-ultra.md))
- **design-ultra-draft**: Internal to design-ultra — Phase 3. Drafts both WHAT (requirements-only) and HOW (implementation-ready) layers of a design doc in two internal phases. Returns section bodies as markdown. Never writes files or assembles the full document. ([tianhuil/dotfiles](https://github.com/tianhuil/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/home/opencode/.config/opencode/agents/design-ultra-draft.md))
- **design-ultra-gap**: Internal to design-ultra — Phase 1. Read-only pre-planning gap analyzer (Metis role). Returns gaps, glossary candidates, and dependency-ordered interview questions. Never writes files or talks to the user. ([tianhuil/dotfiles](https://github.com/tianhuil/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/home/opencode/.config/opencode/agents/design-ultra-gap.md))
- **design-ultra-review**: Internal to design-ultra — Phase 4. Read-only adversarial reviewer (Momus role). Runs 7 lenses and returns P0/P1/P2 findings with a single APPROVE/REJECT verdict. Never writes files. ([tianhuil/dotfiles](https://github.com/tianhuil/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/home/opencode/.config/opencode/agents/design-ultra-review.md))
- **design-unit-analyst**: Analyzes one design unit (screen, region, component, or flow) from a Claude Design export against the current web-ui codebase ("state vs. spec"). Classifies the unit, itemizes visual/behavioral/component-divergence/backend-gap/token-drift changes, maps added UI elements to existing Storybook components or net-new proposals, and writes a schema-validated findings.json. Read-only with respect to both codebases; never implements changes. ([closedloop-ai/claude-plugins](https://github.com/closedloop-ai/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/code/agents/design-unit-analyst.md) · ★124)
- **design-update**: &gt; 注記: 「名前」は思考様式の参照ラベル。本人を名乗らず、方法論のみ適用する。 ([daishiman/harness-dev](https://github.com/daishiman/harness-dev/blob/HEAD/doc/%E5%8F%82%E8%80%83Skill/skill-creator/agents/design-update.md) · ★10 · 4 copies)
- **design-update-checker**: Cold-path enrichment agent for /gdd:check-update --prompt. Reads .design/update-cache.json plus a release body supplied in the prompt, classifies the delta (major|minor|patch|off-cadence), and returns a 3-5-line human-friendly "what this release changes for you" summary.
